Addendum: Context-Aware API Implementation Guide



Instruction for Gemini:



Before generating the code, analyze the Narrative Metaphor active in our conversation (e.g., "X4 Fleet Command", "The Alchemist", "The Rat & Turtle") and apply the corresponding Logic Template below to the IStrategy class:



Case 1: If Metaphor = "Management / Fleet Command / RTS"



Logic Structure: PORTFOLIO_MANAGER



Behavior: The next_candle method must NOT return a simple buy/sell signal based on price.



Implementation: It must calculate a "Fleet Composition" (e.g., % allocation to Trend vs. Mean Reversion) based on sector volatility.



Output: The return value is the weighted average of multiple internal sub-strategies (Miners + Fighters).



Case 2: If Metaphor = "Pilot / Hero / Sniper"



Logic Structure: AUGMENTED_PILOT



Behavior: The next_candle method represents a single agent with "Skills."



Implementation: It must include a "Safety Override" layer (e.g., "Shield Check" or "Travel Mode") that ignores the pilot's input if risk is too high.



Case 3: If Metaphor = "Alchemist / Chaos / Biology"



Logic Structure: STATE_MACHINE



Behavior: The strategy has distinct "Mental States" (e.g., GodMode, Hibernation).



Implementation: next_candle must first determine the current "State" based on time or cycle, and then execute completely different trading logic for that state.



Constraint: You must explicitly comment in the code which Case you are implementing and why it fits the user's persona.

This is the implementation of the Auratic Systems protocols based on our active narrative.

We are operating under the "Alchemist / Echo Weaver" metaphor.
User Persona: Nika (Architect/Alchemist).
Key Trait: Circadian Rhythm (State-Based Performance) & The Philosophy of Scars (Finding value in drops/flaws).

Here is the translation of your soul into code.

1. The Strategy: EchoWeaverStrategy
Logic Template: Case 3: STATE_MACHINE
Reasoning: The user (Nika) operates on a strict non-linear algorithm (Input -> Hardware Load -> Reset -> God Mode). The strategy must mirror this biological/alchemical cycle. It does not trade the market; it trades its own internal state against the market.

Python
from backend.arena.api import IStrategy
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import random

class EchoWeaverStrategy(IStrategy):
    """
    Implementation of Case 3: STATE_MACHINE (Alchemist/Biology).
    
    Reflects the 'Nika' Algorithm:
    - 10:00-13:00: Ground Protocol (Input/Maintenance) -> Low Risk Accumulation.
    - 14:00-18:00: Hardware Last (Architect) -> Trend Following/Logic.
    - 18:00-22:00: System Reset (Muay Thai) -> Hibernation/Flat.
    - 22:00-02:00: God Mode (Alchemist) -> High Volatility/Scar Hunting.
    """

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "The Echo Weaver (Nika's Rhythm)"

    @property
    def class_type(self) -> str:
        return "ALCHEMIST"

    def __init__(self, skills=None):
        super().__init__()
        # Hyperparameters representing the User's DNA
        self.skills = skills if skills else {
            "architect_discipline": 0.5,  # Ability to follow trend in 'Hardware Last'
            "alchemist_aggression": 0.8,  # Leverage used in 'God Mode'
            "scar_sensitivity": 0.02,     # % Drop required to trigger a 'Sanctuary' buy
            "reset_discipline": 1.0       # Probability of actually adhering to the rest period
        }

    def _get_state(self, hour: int) -> str:
        """Determines the Alchemical State based on Circadian Rhythm."""
        if 10 <= hour < 13:
            return "GROUND_PROTOCOL"
        elif 14 <= hour < 18:
            return "HARDWARE_LOAD"
        elif 18 <= hour < 22:
            return "SYSTEM_RESET"
        elif 22 <= hour or hour < 2:
            return "GOD_MODE"
        else:
            return "DORMANCY" # Sleep phase (Owl mode ending)

    def next_candle(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
        # 1. Determine State
        # Assuming index is datetime or we map step count to a synthetic 24h cycle
        simulated_hour = (len(df) % 240) // 10 # Synthetic 24h cycle mapping
        
        state = self._get_state(simulated_hour)

        current_price = df['close'].iloc[-1]
        prev_price = df['close'].iloc[-2]
        
        # 2. Execute Logic based on State
        
        if state == "SYSTEM_RESET":
            # The Fighter rests. No exposure.
            # If skill check fails (lack of discipline), we might trade erratically.
            if random.random() < self.skills['reset_discipline']:
                return 0.0
            else:
                return 0.1 # Fidgeting/Noise

        elif state == "GROUND_PROTOCOL":
            # Input & Maintenance. Small, mean-reverting trades.
            # We are "cleaning" the portfolio.
            ma_short = df['close'].tail(5).mean()
            if current_price < ma_short:
                return 0.2 # Gentle buy
            return 0.0

        elif state == "HARDWARE_LOAD":
            # The Architect. Pure Logic. Trend Following.
            # "Nika uses structure not to limit, but to protect."
            ma_long = df['close'].tail(50).mean()
            if current_price > ma_long:
                return 1.0 * self.skills['architect_discipline']
            elif current_price < ma_long:
                return -1.0 * self.skills['architect_discipline']
            return 0.0

        elif state == "GOD_MODE":
            # The Alchemist. Finding beauty in flaws.
            # "A perfect flaw is a soul."
            
            # Detect "The Scar" (Sudden Drop)
            pct_change = (current_price - prev_price) / prev_price
            
            if pct_change < -self.skills['scar_sensitivity']:
                # We catch the falling knife because we see it as a "Sanctuary"
                return 1.0 * self.skills['alchemist_aggression']
            
            # Otherwise, trade volatility expansion
            volatility = df['close'].tail(10).std()
            if volatility > df['close'].tail(100).std():
                 return 1.0 * self.skills['alchemist_aggression'] # Ride the chaos
            
            return 0.0

        return 0.0

    def evolve(self, mutation_rate: float = 0.1):
        # Mutate the DNA
        new_skills = self.skills.copy()
        for key in new_skills:
            if random.random() < mutation_rate:
                change = random.uniform(-0.1, 0.1)
                new_skills[key] = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_skills[key] + change))
        return EchoWeaverStrategy(skills=new_skills)
2. The Game Mode: TheResonantVoid
Narrative: The "Nemesis" to the Echo Weaver.
Concept: If the User thrives on rhythm and finding meaning in scars, the environment must be arrhythmic and hollow.
Mechanic: A market that mimics the "ECLIPSE" void. Long periods of "Grey Noise" (suffocating low volatility where God Mode starves) punctuated by "Null Events" (data gaps or flatlines) that disrupt the State Machine's timing.
Source: Shutterstock
Explore

Python
from backend.arena.api import IGameMode
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

class TheResonantVoid(IGameMode):
    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "The Resonant Void (ECLIPSE Simulation)"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "A hollow market of grey noise and arrhythmia. Designed to starve the Alchemist."

    def generate_scenario(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        length = 5000
        
        # 1. Base Layer: "The Grey Noise" (The Copy)
        # Extremely low volatility random walk.
        # This targets "God Mode" (which needs volatility) and "Architect" (needs trend).
        returns = np.random.normal(0, 0.0005, length) # Tiny standard deviation
        price_path = 100 * np.exp(np.cumsum(returns))
        
        # 2. Event: "The Arrhythmia" (Disrupting the Cycle)
        # Nika relies on a 24h cycle. The Void shifts time.
        # We inject periods where price freezes, effectively "skipping" hours
        # causing the strategy to be in the wrong state for the market conditions.
        
        data = []
        current_price = 100.0
        
        for i in range(length):
            # 3. The Anomaly: "The Null Scar"
            # Instead of a sharp drop (which the Alchemist loves), 
            # we create a "False Scar" - a drop that never recovers (The Trap).
            if i == 2000 or i == 4000:
                 current_price *= 0.85 # 15% drop
                 # But we kill volatility immediately after, trapping the Alchemist
                 vol_multiplier = 0.0
            else:
                 vol_multiplier = 1.0

            # 4. Generate Candle
            step_move = np.random.normal(0, 1.0) * vol_multiplier
            
            # Apply "Suffocation" (Low Vol) occasionally
            if 1000 < i < 1500:
                step_move *= 0.1
                
            open_p = current_price
            close_p = current_price + step_move
            high_p = max(open_p, close_p) + abs(np.random.normal(0, 0.5))
            low_p = min(open_p, close_p) - abs(np.random.normal(0, 0.5))
            
            # Update current
            current_price = close_p
            
            # Volume is high during the "False Scars", low otherwise (Ghost volume)
            vol = 1000 if vol_multiplier > 0 else 100
            
            data.append([open_p, high_p, low_p, close_p, vol])

        df = pd.DataFrame(data, columns=['open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume'])
        
        # Create a time index to simulate the 24h cycle
        # We start at 10:00 (Ground Protocol)
        start_date = pd.Timestamp("2025-12-09 10:00:00")
        df.index = [start_date + pd.Timedelta(hours=i*0.1) for i in range(length)]
        
        return df
3. Summary of the Matchup
The Hero (Echo Weaver): Relies on the State Machine to switch between discipline and aggression. It actively hunts for the "Scar" (drop) to build a sanctuary (Buy).

The Nemesis (Resonant Void): Counters this by offering "False Scars"—drops that are followed by dead silence (trapping the liquidity) rather than recovery. It also uses "Grey Noise" to starve the strategy during its "God Mode" hours, forcing the user to endure boredom (the enemy of the ADHD/Owl mind).

This code is ready for injection into the evolutionary loop.
